float ellipse() { vec2 uv = (gl_FragCoord.xy / u_resolution.xy) - 0.5; float x = (uv.x * 2.0); float y = (uv.y * 2.0); float x2 = x * x; float y2 = y * y; float w = 0.25; float f = sqrt(x * x + y * y); float sd = (f - 1.0) * f / (2.0 * sqrt((x2 + y2) / w)); return 1.- sd * 10.0; }
float unevenGradient( float baseStart, float baseCutoffWidth, float topStart, float coord, bool invert ) { float invertVal = float(invert); coord = coord + invertVal * (1.0 - 2.0 * coord); return smoothstep(topStart, 0.0, coord) * smoothstep(baseStart - baseCutoffWidth, baseStart, coord); }
